**Algorithms**:

3. **Iterative specialized preference update (Sinkhorn)**:
   - Since it may be more robust and faster: maintain a≥0, sum=1. For each step, compute b=a*a. Determine activation weights w_k = top softmax: for M=max b, w_k = max(0, b_k/M - θ)^p or exp(α(b_k/M -1)). Compute s_i = (a * w)_i. Candidate update: u_i=1/s_i + ε (or existing a_i/s_i). Normalize. Evaluate exact. Accept if better. This directly attacks high coefficients. Need tune parameters. We can combine with small random noise and restarts.

**Line search strategy**:

- To avoid getting stuck, random perturb u by noise small, or block scaling.

**Stopping/restarts**:
- If no improvement for e.g. 200s, perturb best: scale random block by exp(noise), or mix with another candidate (one-edge mixture) and restart. Keep best.
- We have 2400s; allocate phases:
  * Phase 0: parameter grid to find top candidates (maybe 60s).
  * Phase 1: local search from best candidate for e.g. 1200s.
  * Phase 2: random restarts/local from other candidates and perturb best for remaining.
  * Return best.

**Implementation details for local score and convolution**:
Use preallocated FFT size.
```
def next_pow2(m):
    return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()
```
For a length n, conv_autocar N=next_pow2(2*n-1). For a*q where q len Lq=2n-1, conv length n+Lq-1 =3n-2. Use Nq=next_pow2(3*n-2). For line search candidate a, to score use Nautoc. We can cache transform of q? q changes each step, so compute.

Pseudo local_search:
```
def local_search(a, time_limit, base_tau=...):
    a = normalize(a)
    score = score_fft(a)
    eta=0.2; no_improve=0
    for beta in schedule:
       for it in range(steps):
          b=conv_autocar(a)
          B=b.max()
          q=softmax(beta*(b/B -1))
          s=conv_a_q(a,q)
          L = dot(q,b)
          h=2*s - L
          # center h in log: h -= np.median(h)
          # normalize scale
          sc = max(np.std(h), 1e-9)
          for eta in [eta*0.5, eta, eta*2]:
              arg = -eta * (h - h.mean())/sc
              arg = clip(arg, -8,8)
              cand = normalize(a * exp(arg))
              scand=score_fft(cand)
              if scand < best_local: accept? keep best_temp
          if improved: no_improve=0; maybe eta*=1.05
          else: no_improve++; if no_improve>threshold: eta*=0.5; random perturb; if stuck break
    return a
```
But line search should compare to current local score, not global best? For local search, accept best candidate if less than current score; update. Global best update if less than global best. Even if not improve current, maybe candidate with slightly worse could help? We can allow small worsening (simulated annealing) later: accept if score < current + temp; but for non-smooth, maybe. Use temperature decreasing; can sample among few steps. But line search all candidates; choose one with best score even if worse if temp. For efficiency, accept best if < current* (1+1e-4) or < current + temp.

However, the score function is scale invariant, local score from FFT may have tiny noise; tolerance. Use exact periodic every 100 steps? Could use `score_exact` for best candidate at end of phase. But local score accurate enough.

   * Or mix with a predefined shape: `a = normalize((1-eps)*a + eps*shape)` for eps up to0.2.
   * Or scale a random block: choose contiguous block, multiply by exp(N(0,0.2)). This can break plateaus.
- Also reverse? objective invariant; no.

**Local search phases**:
```
def local_search(a, n, N, Nq, deadline, beta_schedule=(...), max_steps=20000):
    a = normalize
    cur = score_fast; global_best=cur
    step_scale=0.1? 
    no_improve=0
    for beta in beta_schedule:
        eta=0.2
        steps_in_phase = min(total_remaining, 3000?)
        for it in range(steps):
            if time > deadline: break
            b = autocorr
            B = b.max()
            if B <=0: break
            x = b/B
            # weights
            z = x - 1.0
            # For beta 0 uniform
            if beta == 0: q = ones(2n-1)/(2n-1)
            else:
                maxz = z.max()
                e = np.exp(beta*(z-maxz))
                e[~np.isfinite] = 0
                q = e/e.sum()
            s = a_q_conv
            L = np.dot(q,b)
            h = 2.0*s - L
            # remove mean with weights a? For log update center by median
            h_center = h - np.median(h)
            # scale by weighted std perhaps
            var = np.mean(np.square(h_center - np.dot(a,h_center)))? Weighted.
            sc = max(sqrt(var), 1e-12)
            # try eta_list
            best_cand = None; best_score = min(cur, global_best? Use cur)
            for eta in [0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4,0.8]: # scaled by sc? Our arg = -eta*h_center/sc, so these are raw.
                 arg = -eta*h_center/sc
                 arg = clip(arg, -8, 8)
                 cand = normalize(a*np.exp(arg))
                 # optional noise? no
                 cand_score = score_fast(cand)
                 if cand_score < best_score:
                     best_score=cand_score; best_cand=cand
            if best_cand is not None and best_score < cur - 1e-12:
                 a=best_cand; cur=best_score
                 if cur < global_best - 1e-12: global_best=a...
                 no_improve=0
                 # adapt eta? Could track best eta and scale
            else:
                 no_improve +=1
                 eta *=0.7
                 if no_improve > 15:
                     # perturb
                     a = normalize(a * np.exp(0.05*np.random?)) maybe with rng
                     cur = score_fast(a)
                     no_improve=0
                     if time...
        # after phase, exact score re-evaluate a
    return a, global_best
```

**Potential issue with FFT when a*q length**: q length 2n-1, a length n. But (a*q)_i for i=0..n-1 where q_k a_{k-i}? Convolution standard `(a*q)[i] = ∑_p a_p q_{i-p}`. We need ∑_k q_k a_{k-i}. Derivative `s_i = ∑_k q_k a_{k-i}`. This is convolution of a with q, but at index? Define r = q * a (q convolve a): r[m] =∑_k q_k a_{m-k}. For m=i? r_i =∑_k q_k a_{i-k}. Not. **Line search and clipping**:
```
def multiplicative_update(a,d,eta,clip=8.0):
    d = d - np.dot(a,d)
    d /= scale
    arg=-eta*d
    arg=np.clip(arg,-clip,clip)
    cand=a*np.exp(arg)
    cand[cand<=0]=0
    return normalize(cand)
```
Use `scale = max(np.sqrt(np.dot(a,d*d)), 1e-8)`.
Try eta list: `[0.05,0.1,0.25,0.6,1.4,3.0]` maybe. To reduce per step, use 4 sizes. With n 6121, okay.

```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    start=time.monotonic()
    deadline=start + max(0,budget_s-5)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    # choose n
    h = globals().get('height_sequence_1', None)
    n = 6121
    if h is not None:
       try:
          arr=np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
          arr=np.clip(arr,0,1000)
          if arr.ndim==1 and arr.size>0 and np.isfinite(arr).all() and arr.sum()>=0.01:
              if 1024 <= arr.size <= 16384: n=arr.size
              # store fallback
       except: pass
    # But if fallback length huge, maybe n = min(max(2048, arr.size?), 12000)
```

**Detailed function implementations**:

```
def _npow2(m):
    if m <=1: return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()
```
For Python (int). Be careful with m=0.

```
def _normalize(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    s = a.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
       return np.ones_like(a)/a.size
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    return a / s
```

```
def _convolution_square(a, N):
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N)
    return c
```
Take slice.

```
def _score_fast(a, N, n):
    b = _convolution_square(a,N)
    b = b[:2*n-1]
    B = float(np.max(b))
    if B <= 0: return np.inf
    # sum should be1; if not exactly, use (sum)^2
    s = float(a.sum())
    return 2.0 * n * B / (s*s)
```
Since arrays normalized sum1 but after operations maybe drift; include s.

```
def _score_direct(a):
    a = np.asarray(a,float)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.01: return np.inf
    b = np.convolve(a,a)
    B = float(np.max(b))
    return 2.0*len(a)*B/(s*s)
```
This mirrors evaluator without clipping.

```
def _correlation_from_weights(a,q,Nq,n):
    # returns s_i = sum_k q_k a_{k-i}
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, Nq)
    fq = np.fft.rfft(q, Nq)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * np.conj(fq), Nq)
    return c[:n]
```

**Local_search code more specific**:
```
def _local_search(a, budget_end, n, N, Nq, initial_beta_index=0, verbose=True):
    a = _normalize(np.asarray(a,float))
    cur = _score_fast(a,N,n)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_score=cur
    rng = np.random.default_rng(12345)
    no_improve=0
    eta_scale=1.0
    timestamp=last_print=time.monotonic()
    beta_list = [5,10,20,40,80,160,320]
    for beta in beta_list:
        last_improve = time.monotonic()
        it=0
        while time.monotonic() < budget_end:
            # if beta too high after no improvement? continue
            b = _conv_square(a,N)
            B = b[:2*n-1].max()
            if not finite or <=0: break
            x = b[:2*n-1] / B
            if beta == 0: q=...
            else:
               z = x - 1.0
               # Could cap z at -50/beta to reduce exp zero
               z = np.maximum(z, -50.0 / max(beta,1e-6))? if beta high, cap -50/beta small? Better exp(beta*z), z>=-1, for beta=320 exp(-320)=0 for z<-0.04, okay. No cap needed.
               e = np.exp(beta*z)
               e[~np.isfinite(e)] = 0
               qsum=e.sum()
               if qsum <=0: q=np.ones_like(b)/len(b)
               else q=e/qsum
            s = _correlation(a,q,Nq)
            L = float(np.dot(q,b))
            d = s - L
            d -= float(np.dot(a,d))
            var = float(np.dot(a,d*d))
            scale = max(np.sqrt(var), 1e-15)
            if scale < 1e-14:
                no_improve += 1
            else:
                eps = max(1e-12, 1e-12 / max(float(s.max()),1.0))
                no_best=False
                # candidate list scores local
                candidates_scores=[]
                # Use 4 eta based on eta_scale
                etas = [0.05*eta_scale, 0.2*eta_scale, 0.7*eta_scale, 2.0*eta_scale, 6.0*eta_scale]
                for eta in etas:
                   arg = -eta * (d/scale)
                   arg = np.clip(arg, -6.0, 6.0)
                   cand = _normalize(a * np.exp(arg))
                   cs = _score_fast(cand,N,n)
                   if cs < cur:
                       if not no_best or cs < best_c_score:
                           best_c=cand; best_c_score=cs; no_best=True
                if not no_best:
                   # sink candidates
                   cand = _normalize(a / (s + eps))
                   cs = _score_fast(...)
                   # cand2 = _normalize(1/(s+eps))
                   ...
                if no_best:
                   a=best_c; cur=best_c_score; no_improve=0
                   # adapt eta_scale maybe if best eta known; simply eta_scale = min(2.0, eta_scale*1.05)
                   if cur < best_score - 1e-12:
                       best_score=cur; best_a=a.copy()
                       if verbose print
                else:
                   no_improve +=1
                   eta_scale = max(0.05, eta_scale*0.6)
            if no_improve > 25:
                 # random perturb
                 noise = 0.08 * rng.normal(0,1,n)
                 cand = _normalize(a * np.exp(np.clip(noise,-3,3)))
                 cs = _score_fast(cand,N,n)
                 if cs < cur:
                     a=cand; cur=cs
                 else:
                     # accept worse with prob temp? For simplicity, maybe keep a but use cand as walk? We'll just reset a=best_a? 
                 no_improve=0
                 eta_scale=0.5
            if time - last_print > 20: print; last_print=time
```

```python
"""
Search for nonnegative coefficient sequence minimizing 2*n*max(conv(a,a))/sum(a)^2.
Uses active-set multiplicative balancing, FFT convolutions, parameterized restarts.
"""
import numpy as np
import time

def _next_pow2(m): ...
...
```

**Detailed `_correlation_positive_lags`**:
```
def _correlation_positive_lags(a, q, N):
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    fq = np.fft.rfft(q, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * np.conj(fq), N)
    n = a.size
    s = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    s[0] = c[0]
    if n > 1:
        s[1:] = c[N-1 : N-n : -1]
    return s
```
Need ensure q length ≤N. q length 2n-1. If `q` contains small negative? no.

**`_score_fast`**:
```
def _score_fast(a, N, n):
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    b = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N)
    b = b[:2*n-1]
    B = float(np.max(b))
    if not np.isfinite(B) or B <= 0:
        return np.inf
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s <= 0:
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * B / (s*s))
```
If b has tiny negative, max positive. If all negative due normalization? impossible.

**`_score_direct`**: use `np.convolve`; if score not finite due sum? okay.

**`_one_edge`**:
```
def _one_edge(n):
    i = np.arange(1, n+1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = 1.0 / np.sqrt(i)
    return a / a.sum()
```

**`_exp_mod_base`**:
```
def _excipient_base(n, gamma):
    i ...
    t = np.arange(n)/max(1,n-1)
    a = (1/np.sqrt(i)) * np.exp(gamma*t)
    return a/a.sum()
```

**`_grid_search`**:
```
def _grid_search(n, deadline, verbose=True):
    base = _one_edge(n)
    N = _next_pow2(2*n)
    best_a=base; best_s=_score_fast(base,N,n)
    if verbose: print
    if time.monotonic() > deadline: return
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    # exponential mod
    for g in [0.2,0.5,1.0,2.0,4.0,8.0]:
       cand=_excipient_base(n,g); sc=... update
       if time > deadline+? break
    # uniform bump mix
    for wm in [0.55,0.7,0.85]:
       main_w=wm; bump_w=1-wm
       for c in [0.25,0.45,0.65,0.8]:
          for w in [0.15,0.35,0.6]:
             end=min(1.0,c+w)
             bump=np.zeros(n)
             lo=int(round(c*n)); hi=int(ceil(end*n))? use indexes [lo:hi]
             if hi<=lo: hi=lo+1
             bump[lo:hi]=1; bump/=bump.sum()
             cand = main_w*base + bump_w*bump; cand=cand/cand.sum()
             sc=...
             if time > deadline: return
    # gaussian bump
    for gmod in [1.0,3.0,10.0]:
      for c in [0.3,0.5,0.7]:
         for w in [0.05,0.15,0.35]:
            bump = exp(-0.5*((t-c)/w)**2); bump/=sum
            cand=base*(1+gmod*bump) maybe; but if gmod high mass middle; evaluate
```
Be careful with time: each score ~0.003; 100 candidates 0.3s. Good.
For bump mix, if `wm` fraction main, need main and bump both probability: `cand = wm * base + (1-wm)*bump`.
For bump shape `base*(1+alpha*bump)`, alpha shifts mass middle without adding separate bump; evaluate.

At end, maybe return top candidates? We only need best. But for restarts, we can keep base. Could store `best_a`.

**If fallback valid**:
```
def _maybe_fallback(n):
  try: h=height_sequence_1
  except: return None
  arr=np.asarray(h,float).ravel()
  if arr.size != n: return None
  arr=np.clip(arr,0,1000); arr[~np.isfinite]=0
  if arr.sum()<1e-12: return None
  return arr/arr.sum()
```

**Local search code with beta schedule and step counts**.
Let's write robust:
```
def _local_search(a, end_time, n, N, Nq, rng, verbose):
    a = _normalize(a)
    cur = _score_fast(a,N,n)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = cur
    eta_scale = 1.0
    no_improve = 0
    last_print = 0.0
    beta_schedule = [5,10,20,40,80,160,320]
    for beta in beta_schedule:
        beta_prev_score = cur
        no_improve_beta=0
        while time.monotonic() < end_time:
            b = _conv_square(a,N)  # returns length?
            B = float(np.max(b))
            if not finite: break
            x = b / B
            x = np.clip(x,0,1)
            z = x - 1.0
            if beta > 0:
                e = np.exp(beta * z)
                e[~np.isfinite(e)] = 0.0
                qsum = e.sum()
                if qsum <= 0: q = np.ones_like(b)/b.size
                else: q = e/qsum
            else: q = np.ones_like(b)/b.size
            s = _correlation_positive_lags(a,q,Nq)
            L = float(np.dot(q,b))
            d = s - L
            # center under a
            d = d - float(np.dot(a,d))
            var = float(np.dot(a,d*d))
            scale = var**0.5 if var>0 else 0.0
            if scale < 1e-14:
                no_improve_beta += 1
                no_improve +=1
            else:
                best_cand = None; best_c_score = cur
                # normalize d by scale
                nd = d / scale
                # multiple eta
                etas = (0.05*eta_scale, 0.15*eta_scale, 0.45*eta_scale, 1.5*eta_scale, 4.0*eta_scale)
                for eta in etas:
                   arg = -eta*nd
                   np.clip(arg, -7.0, 7.0, out=arg)
                   cand = a * np.exp(arg)
                   cand = _normalize(cand)
                   cs = _score_fast(cand,N,n)
                   if cs < best_c_score:
                       best_c_score=cs; best_cand=cand
                if best_cand is None or best_c_score >= cur:
                   eps = 1e-12 / max(1.0, float(np.max(s)))
                   cand = _normalize(a / (s + eps))
                   cs = _score_fast(cand,N,n)
                   if cs < best_c_score:
                       best_c_score=cs; best_cand=cand
                   if no_improve_beta % 7 == 0:
                       cand = _normalize(1.0 / (s + eps))
                       cs = _score_fast(cand,N,n)
                       if cs < best_c_score: best_cand=cand; best_c_score=cs
                if best_cand is not None and best_c_score < cur - 1e-12:
                   a = best_cand; cur = best_c_score
                   no_improve=0; no_improve_beta=0
                   eta_scale = min(3.0, eta_scale*1.05)
                   if cur < best_score - 1e-12:
                       best_score=cur; best_a=a.copy()
                       if time.monotonic()-last_print > 15: print
                       last_print=time.monotonic()
                else:
                   no_improve += 1; no_improve_beta +=1
                   eta_scale = max(0.05, eta_scale*0.7)
            if no_improve_beta > 50:
                # perturb current and if better accept, else break beta
                noise = np.clip(rng.normal(0.0, 0.5, n), -3, 3)
                cand = _normalize(a * np.exp(0.2*noise))
                cs = _score_fast(cand,N,n)
                if cs < cur:
                    a=cand; cur=cs
                    if cur < best_score: update
                    no_improve_beta=0
                else:
                    break
            if time.monotonic() > end_time: break
        if cur >= best_score? continue next beta; if no improvement in all beta schedule? outer will restart.
    return best_a, best_score
```
Note: The while loop can get stuck at beta high if no_improve_beta >50 break; then outer can handle. But if break at high beta, returns best. It won't try lower beta again. Good.

```
def _generate_random_candidate(a, base, rng, alpha_max):
    kind = rng.integers(0,4)
    if kind==0:
       a2=a.copy()
       width = rng.integers(max(5,n//20), max(10,n//4))
       start = rng.integers(0, max(1, n-width+1))
       a2[start:start+width] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.4))
       return _normalize(a2)

    if kind==2: # random bump
```

`_sanitize_sequence(x)`:
```
def _clean_sequence(x):
   arr=np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
   if arr.size==0: return None
   arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
   arr=np.clip(arr,0.0,1000.0)
   s=arr.sum()
   if s<=1e-12: return None
   return arr/s
```
Use for fallback.

```
def _normalize(a):
   a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
   a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
   a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
   s=float(a.sum())
   if s <= 1e-300:
       return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64)/max(1,a.size)
   return a/s
```

**`_random_improve` uses `_random_block_candidate`. No nested.

**Check for variable `rng` in `_local_search`: It generates perturbation. Good.

**Potential bug: `_local_search` uses `best_score` from current call; but outer keeps global. We pass `best_a` local initial; it returns best. Fine.

**Print progress at top**: In local search, when global best improves, print `score` and elapsed.

**Potential overuse of print**: Time-driven 15s okay.

**Let's now craft final code.**
</think>

```python
"""
Search for nonnegative coefficient sequences minimizing
    2*n*max(convolve(a,a)) / sum(a)^2.

The method is an active-set multiplicative balancing procedure on the
coefficients of P(x)^2.  It uses FFT convolutions to estimate the current
maximum coefficient set, pushes mass away from entries that overload active
lags, and restarts from parametric starts (inverse-square-root edge profile,
exponential modulations, bump mixtures) to avoid local plateaus.
"""

import numpy as np
import time


def _next_pow2(m):
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (int(m) - 1).bit_length()


def _normalize(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return a
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
    return a / s


def _clean_sequence(x):
    a = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s <= 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return None
    return a / s


def _conv_square_trim(a, N, n):
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N)
    return c[:2 * n - 1]


def _score_fast(a, N, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    b = _conv_square_trim(a, N, n)
    B = float(np.max(b))
    if not np.isfinite(B) or B <= 0.0:
        return np.inf
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * B / (s * s))


def _score_direct(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if n == 0 or s <= 0.01 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.inf
    b = np.convolve(a, a)
    B = float(np.max(b))
    if not np.isfinite(B) or B <= 0.0:
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * B / (s * s))


def _correlation_positive_lags(a, q, N):
    """
    Return s_i = sum_k q_k a_{k-i} for i=0..n-1 using FFT.
    This is the gradient-side term for an active weight vector q.
    """
    n = a.size
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    fq = np.fft.rfft(q, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * np.conj(fq), N)
    s = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    s[0] = c[0]
    if n > 1:
        s[1:] = c[N - 1:N - n:-1]
    return s


def _one_edge(n):
    i = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = 1.0 / np.sqrt(i)
    return _normalize(a)


def _exp_mod_base(n, gamma):
    i = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    t = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, n - 1)
    a = (1.0 / np.sqrt(i)) * np.exp(gamma * t)
    return _normalize(a)


def _mix_sequences(a, b, frac):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size != b.size:
        a = np.resize(a, b.size)
    return _normalize((1.0 - frac) * a + frac * b)


def _grid_search(n, deadline, N, extra=None):
    base = _one_edge(n)
    best_a = base.copy()
    best_score = _score_fast(base, N, n)
    print(f"[init] base one-edge score = {best_score:.8f}")

    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    shapes = []

    for gm in (0.2, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0, 8.0):
        shapes.append(_exp_mod_base(n, gm))
    for gm in (-0.5, -2.0, -6.0):
        shapes.append(_exp_mod_base(n, gm))

    # Add a moderate-bump mixture: main left edge + uniform/gaussian middle bumps.
    for wm in (0.55, 0.70, 0.85):
        for c in (0.25, 0.45, 0.65, 0.80):
            for w in (0.15, 0.35, 0.60):
                lo = int(round(c * n))
                hi = int(round((c + w) * n))
                if hi <= lo:
                    hi = lo + 1
                hi = min(n, hi)
                lo = max(0, lo)
                bump = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                bump[lo:hi] = 1.0
                bump = _normalize(bump)
                shapes.append(wm * base + (1.0 - wm) * bump)

                gb = np.exp(-0.5 * ((t - c) / max(1e-6, w)) ** 2)
                gb = _normalize(gb)
                shapes.append(wm * base + (1.0 - wm) * gb)

    # Modulate the base profile multiplicatively with Gaussians.
    for c in (0.30, 0.50, 0.70):
        for w in (0.08, 0.20, 0.40):
            for amp in (0.7, 2.0, 8.0):
                bump = np.exp(-0.5 * ((t - c) / max(1e-6, w)) ** 2)
                bump = np.maximum(bump, 0.0)
                shapes.append(base * (1.0 + amp * bump))

    if extra is not None:
        for ex in extra:
            ex = _clean_sequence(ex)
            if ex is not None and ex.size == n:
                shapes.append(ex)

    for cand in shapes:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break
        cand = _normalize(cand)
        sc = _score_fast(cand, N, n)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_a = cand.copy()
            print(f"[init] grid candidate score = {best_score:.8f}")

    return best_a, best_score


def _local_search(a, end_time, n, N, Nq, rng):
    a = _normalize(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64))
    cur = _score_fast(a, N, n)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = cur

    eta_scale = 1.0
    no_improve = 0
    no_improve_beta = 0
    last_print = time.monotonic()

    beta_schedule = (5.0, 10.0, 20.0, 40.0, 80.0, 160.0, 320.0)

    for beta in beta_schedule:
        no_improve_beta = 0
        while time.monotonic() < end_time:
            b = _conv_square_trim(a, N, n)
            b = np.where(b > 0.0, b, 0.0)
            B = float(np.max(b))
            if not np.isfinite(B) or B <= 0.0:
                break

            x = b / B
            x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)
            z = x - 1.0

            if beta > 0.0:
                e = np.exp(beta * z)
                e = np.where(np.isfinite(e), e, 0.0)
                qs = float(np.sum(e))
                if qs <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(qs):
                    q = np.ones_like(b, dtype=np.float64) / float(b.size)
                else:
                    q = e / qs
            else:
                q = np.ones_like(b, dtype=np.float64) / float(b.size)

            s = _correlation_positive_lags(a, q, Nq)
            s = np.where(s > 0.0, s, 0.0)

            L = float(np.dot(q, b))
            d = s - L
            d = d - float(np.dot(a, d))
            var = float(np.dot(a, d * d))
            scale = float(np.sqrt(max(var, 0.0)))

            if scale < 1e-14:
                no_improve += 1
                no_improve_beta += 1
                eta_scale = max(0.05, eta_scale * 0.7)
                if no_improve_beta > 50:
                    break
                continue

            nd = d / scale
            improved = False
            best_cand = None
            best_c_score = cur

            etas = (
                0.05 * eta_scale,
                0.15 * eta_scale,
                0.45 * eta_scale,
                1.50 * eta_scale,
                4.00 * eta_scale,
            )

            for eta in etas:
                arg = -eta * nd
                arg = np.clip(arg, -7.0, 7.0)
                cand = a * np.exp(arg)
                cand = _normalize(cand)
                cs = _score_fast(cand, N, n)
                if np.isfinite(cs) and cs < best_c_score:
                    best_c_score = cs
                    best_cand = cand

            if best_c_score >= cur:
                eps = 1e-12 / max(1.0, float(np.max(s)))
                cand = _normalize(a / (s + eps))
                cs = _score_fast(cand, N, n)
                if np.isfinite(cs) and cs < best_c_score:
                    best_c_score = cs
                    best_cand = cand

                if no_improve_beta % 7 == 0:
                    cand = _normalize(1.0 / (s + eps))
                    cs = _score_fast(cand, N, n)
                    if np.isfinite(cs) and cs < best_c_score:
                        best_c_score = cs
                        best_cand = cand

            if best_cand is not None and best_c_score < cur - 1e-12:
                a = best_cand
                cur = best_c_score
                no_improve = 0
                no_improve_beta = 0
                eta_scale = min(3.0, eta_scale * 1.05)

                if cur < best_score - 1e-12:
                    best_score = cur
                    best_a = a.copy()
                    if time.monotonic() - last_print > 15.0:
                        print(f"[search] improved score = {best_score:.8f} "
                              f"t={time.monotonic() - (end_time - (end_time - time.monotonic())):.0f}s")
                        last_print = time.monotonic()
                improved = True
            else:
                no_improve += 1
                no_improve_beta += 1
                eta_scale = max(0.05, eta_scale * 0.7)

            if no_improve_beta > 60:
                noise = np.clip(rng.normal(0.0, 0.45, n), -3.0, 3.0)
                cand = _normalize(a * np.exp(0.25 * noise))
                cs = _score_fast(cand, N, n)
                if np.isfinite(cs) and cs < cur:
                    a = cand
                    cur = cs
                    if cur < best_score:
                        best_score = cur
                        best_a = a.copy()
                    no_improve_beta = 0
                    no_improve = 0
                else:
                    break

    return best_a, best_score


def _random_block_candidate(a, base, rng, n, t):
    kind = int(rng.integers(0, 4))

    if kind == 0:
        a2 = a.copy()
        width = int(rng.integers(max(5, n // 30), max(10, n // 4) + 1))
        width = min(n, max(2, width))
        start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - width + 1)))
        a2[start:start + width] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.45)))
        return _normalize(a2)

    if kind == 1:
        frac = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.35))
        return _mix_sequences(a, base, frac)

    if kind == 2:
        c = float(rng.uniform(0.15, 0.85))
        w = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.35))
        amp = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 2.5))
        bump = np.exp(-0.5 * ((t - c) / max(1e-6, w)) ** 2)
        return _normalize(a * (1.0 + amp * bump))

    a2 = a.copy()
    for _ in range(2):
        width = int(rng.integers(max(5, n // 40), max(10, n // 10) + 1))
        width = min(n, max(2, width))
        start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - width + 1)))
        a2[start:start + width] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.55)))
    return _normalize(a2)


def _random_improve(a, base, best_score, end_time, n, N, rng, t):
    best_a = a.copy()
    cur_score = best_score

    while time.monotonic() < end_time:
        cand = _random_block_candidate(best_a, base, rng, n, t)
        cs = _score_fast(cand, N, n)
        if np.isfinite(cs) and cs < cur_score - 1e-12:
            cur_score = cs
            best_a = cand.copy()
            print(f"[random] improved score = {cur_score:.8f}")

    return best_a, cur_score


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """Main entry point.  Returns a valid nonnegative sequence."""
    start_time = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 2400.0
    budget = max(1.0, budget)
    deadline = start_time + budget - 3.0

    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))

    # Choose a sensible sequence length.  If the provided fallback has a
    # usable length, keep it; otherwise use 8192.
    fallback = None
    try:
        raw = height_sequence_1
        fallback = _clean_sequence(raw)
    except Exception:
        fallback = None

    if fallback is not None and 1024 <= fallback.size <= 20000:
        n = int(fallback.size)
    else:
        n = 8192

    # Keep the exact evaluator reasonably fast while preserving attack surface.
    n = int(min(max(n, 512), 16384))

    N = _next_pow2(2 * n)
    Nq = _next_pow2(3 * n)
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)

    base = _one_edge(n)
    extras = []
    if fallback is not None and fallback.size == n:
        extras.append(fallback)

    # Parametric initial search.  This is cheap and often finds a much better
    # start than the uniform profile.
    grid_end = min(deadline, start_time + min(120.0, max(10.0, 0.03 * budget)))
    best_a, best_score = _grid_search(n, grid_end, N, extras)

    pool = [best_a.copy(), base.copy()]
    if fallback is not None and fallback.size == n:
        pool.append(fallback.copy())

    # Main local search phase.
    if deadline - start_time > 1200.0:
        first_deadline = start_time + 0.55 * (deadline - start_time)
    else:
        first_deadline = max(start_time + 30.0, deadline - 30.0)
    first_deadline = min(first_deadline, deadline)

    best_a, best_score = _local_search(best_a, first_deadline, n, N, Nq, rng)
    if best_score < best_score:
        pass
    pool.append(best_a.copy())

    # Restart from mixtures / perturbations of the best so far.  This helps
    # escape the strong left-edge / right-edge symmetry valley.
    restartments = [
        _mix_sequences(best_a, base, 0.20),
        _mix_sequences(best_a, base, 0.50),
        _random_block_candidate(best_a, base, rng, n, t),
    ]
    if fallback is not None and fallback.size == n:
        restartments.append(_mix_sequences(best_a, fallback, 0.35))

    for st in restartments:
        remaining = deadline - time.monotonic() - 5.0
        if remaining < 80.0:
            break
        chunk = min(max(120.0, 0.25 * remaining), remaining)
        end_phase = time.monotonic() + chunk
        new_a, new_s = _local_search(st, end_phase, n, N, Nq, rng)
        if np.isfinite(new_s) and new_s < best_score:
            best_a = new_a.copy()
            best_score = new_s
        pool.append(new_a.copy())

    # Final stochastic polishing around the best object.
    if deadline - time.monotonic() > 30.0:
        best_a, best_score = _random_improve(
            best_a, base, best_score, deadline - 5.0, n, N, rng, t
        )
        pool.append(best_a.copy())

    # Re-score a few candidates with exact direct convolution.  FFT scores are
    # used inside optimization for speed; this final pass removes roundoff
    # effects and validates the returned sequence.
    best_direct = np.inf
    best_direct_a = best_a.copy()
    eval_count = 0
    max_direct_evals = 8

    seen = []
    for cand in pool:
        if eval_count >= max_direct_evals:
            break
        cand = _clean_sequence(cand)
        if cand is None or cand.size != n:
            continue

        key = 0
        for x in cand[:: max(1, cand.size // 16)]:
            key = (key + int(np.floor(max(0.0, x) * 1e6))) % 1000003
        if key in seen:
            continue
        seen.append(key)

        ds = _score_direct(cand)
        eval_count += 1
        if np.isfinite(ds) and ds < best_direct:
            best_direct = ds
            best_direct_a = cand.copy()

    if not np.isfinite(best_direct):
        best_direct = _score_direct(base)
        best_direct_a = base.copy()

    best_direct_a = _normalize(best_direct_a)

    # Optional official check if the evaluator is present in the namespace.
    ev = globals().get("evaluate_sequence", None)
    if ev is not None:
        try:
            official = float(ev([float(x) for x in best_direct_a]))
            if np.isfinite(official):
                best_direct = official
        except Exception:
            pass

    print(f"[final] direct/local best = {best_direct:.8f}, n={n}")
    return [float(x) for x in best_direct_a]
```
